# Core API reference

> Generated by `ApiDocsGenerator` from [`core-api.openapi.json`](core-api.openapi.json) and examples recorded against the running service. Do not edit by hand; regenerate with `ONELOAF_EXPORT_DOCS=1 dotnet test tests/OneLoaf.IntegrationTests --filter FullyQualifiedName~ApiDocsGenerator`.

**Base URL:** `https://<domain>/core` (production behind Caddy), `http://localhost:5080` (local)  
**Version:** `v1` (in the path) · **Format:** JSON, camelCase, enums as snake_case strings, times ISO 8601 with offset  
**Errors:** RFC 9457 problem details — see [error taxonomy](../guides/errors.md). **Auth:** see [authentication](../guides/authentication.md).

103 operations.

## Kitchen

<a id="getwarnings"></a>
### Data quality warnings

`GET /api/v1/warnings` · operation id `GetWarnings` · auth: bearer token

Missing prices, empty recipes, overdue batches and starters of the current user.

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | array of [WarningDto](#model-warningdto) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — typical (200)</summary>

```http
GET /api/v1/warnings
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

Response `200` `application/json`:

```json
[
  {
    "issueCode": "missing_price",
    "entityType": "ingredient",
    "entityId": "7f24a41b-eaaa-4dfa-84a0-ce5a76612349",
    "details": "No price for \"Whole wheat flour\" used in \"Beginner sourdough\""
  }
]
```

</details>

<a id="getkitchensettings"></a>
### Get the kitchen settings

`GET /api/v1/kitchen-settings` · operation id `GetKitchenSettings` · auth: bearer token

US-13, US-47, US-48, US-49. Oven, bowl, tariff, packaging, loss, labor rate, currency, time zone.

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [KitchenSettingsDto](#model-kitchensettingsdto) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — defaults after registration (200)</summary>

```http
GET /api/v1/kitchen-settings
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

Response `200` `application/json`:

```json
{
  "ovenPowerKw": null,
  "ovenCapacityLoaves": null,
  "mixerBowlCapacityKg": null,
  "electricityTariffPerKwh": null,
  "packagingCostPerLoaf": 0.0000,
  "lossRatePercent": 0.00,
  "laborRatePerHour": null,
  "currency": "EUR",
  "timeZone": "Europe/Vilnius",
  "updatedAt": "2026-11-24T23:58:02.371242+00:00"
}
```

</details>

<a id="updatekitchensettings"></a>
### Replace the kitchen settings

`PUT /api/v1/kitchen-settings` · operation id `UpdateKitchenSettings` · auth: bearer token

US-13, US-47, US-48, US-49. Used by the cost and kneading calculations.

**Request body:** [KitchenSettingsRequest](#model-kitchensettingsrequest)

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [KitchenSettingsDto](#model-kitchensettingsdto) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — typical (200)</summary>

```http
PUT /api/v1/kitchen-settings
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "ovenPowerKw": 2.5,
  "ovenCapacityLoaves": 4,
  "mixerBowlCapacityKg": 4.0,
  "electricityTariffPerKwh": 0.25,
  "packagingCostPerLoaf": 0.15,
  "lossRatePercent": 10,
  "laborRatePerHour": 12,
  "currency": "EUR",
  "timeZone": "Europe/Vilnius"
}
```

Response `200` `application/json`:

```json
{
  "ovenPowerKw": 2.50,
  "ovenCapacityLoaves": 4,
  "mixerBowlCapacityKg": 4.00,
  "electricityTariffPerKwh": 0.2500,
  "packagingCostPerLoaf": 0.1500,
  "lossRatePercent": 10.00,
  "laborRatePerHour": 12.00,
  "currency": "EUR",
  "timeZone": "Europe/Vilnius",
  "updatedAt": "2026-11-24T23:58:03.048943+00:00"
}
```

</details>

<details><summary>Example — unknown time zone (400)</summary>

```http
PUT /api/v1/kitchen-settings
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "ovenPowerKw": 2.5,
  "ovenCapacityLoaves": 4,
  "mixerBowlCapacityKg": 4.0,
  "electricityTariffPerKwh": 0.25,
  "packagingCostPerLoaf": 0.15,
  "lossRatePercent": 10,
  "laborRatePerHour": 12,
  "currency": "EUR",
  "timeZone": "Mars/Olympus"
}
```

Response `400` `application/problem+json`:

```json
{
  "type": "https://oneloaf.app/problems/validation",
  "title": "One or more validation errors occurred.",
  "status": 400,
  "instance": "/api/v1/kitchen-settings",
  "errors": {
    "timeZone": [
      "'Time Zone' must be an IANA time zone, for example Europe/Vilnius."
    ]
  },
  "traceId": "00-1b83ae72708c7309c2fd2eaa4634dcd5-671e9f797283d3da-02",

}
```

</details>

## Library

<a id="publishrecipe"></a>
### Publish a recipe to the library

`POST /api/v1/recipes/{id}/publish` · operation id `PublishRecipe` · auth: bearer token

US-39. Publishes an immutable snapshot of the effective recipe; later edits of the recipe do not change it.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 201 | Created; `Location` header points to the new resource | [PublishResponse](#model-publishresponse) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — typical (201)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/recipes/e6e62d43-bafb-462e-8b2d-88853bb427c2/publish
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

Response `201` `application/json`:

```json
{
  "publishedRecipeId": "4183c7fb-b3fc-4f46-b2dc-2ee80e31490a"
}
```

</details>

<details><summary>Example — publish again (new snapshot) (201)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/recipes/e6e62d43-bafb-462e-8b2d-88853bb427c2/publish
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

Response `201` `application/json`:

```json
{
  "publishedRecipeId": "066afb47-331b-4fa8-bb7b-6ffd658d0c6e"
}
```

</details>

<a id="searchlibrary"></a>
### Browse and search the library

`GET /api/v1/library` · operation id `SearchLibrary` · auth: none

US-40. Anonymous. Published recipes whose title contains the search text, newest first; paged and cached for 5 minutes.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `search` | query | string | no | Part of the title. |
| `page` | query | integer (int32), default `1` | no | Page, from 1. |
| `pageSize` | query | integer (int32), default `20` | no | Page size, 1-100. |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [PagedResultOfLibraryItemDto](#model-pagedresultoflibraryitemdto) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — anonymous search (200)</summary>

```http
GET /api/v1/library?search=sourdough&page=1&pageSize=20
```

Response `200` `application/json`:

```json
{
  "items": [
    {
      "id": "4183c7fb-b3fc-4f46-b2dc-2ee80e31490a",
      "title": "Country sourdough",
      "description": "Open crumb, crisp crust.",
      "authorName": "Docs Baker",
      "publishedAt": "2026-11-24T23:58:06.258779+00:00",
      "yieldLoaves": 1,
      "ingredientCount": 4,
      "stageCount": 5,
      "commentCount": 0,
      "reactionCount": 0,
      "hasPhoto": false
    }
  ],
  "page": 1,
  "pageSize": 20,
  "totalCount": 1
}
```

</details>

<a id="getpublication"></a>
### A published recipe

`GET /api/v1/library/{id}` · operation id `GetPublication` · auth: none

US-40. Anonymous. Snapshot, author name, comment and reaction counts. Unpublished ones are visible only to the author.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [PublishedRecipeDto](#model-publishedrecipedto) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — anonymous (200)</summary>

```http
GET /api/v1/library/4183c7fb-b3fc-4f46-b2dc-2ee80e31490a
```

Response `200` `application/json`:

```json
{
  "id": "4183c7fb-b3fc-4f46-b2dc-2ee80e31490a",
  "title": "Country sourdough",
  "description": "Open crumb, crisp crust.",
  "authorName": "Docs Baker",
  "status": "published",
  "publishedAt": "2026-11-24T23:58:06.258779+00:00",
  "snapshot": {
    "stages": [
      {
        "name": "Mix",
        "proofMode": null,
        "stageType": "mix",
        "isOptional": false,
        "maxMinutes": 20,
        "minMinutes": 15,
        "temperatureC": null
      },
      {
        "name": "Bulk fermentation",
        "proofMode": null,
        "stageType": "bulk_fermentation",
        "isOptional": false,
        "maxMinutes": 300,
        "minMinutes": 240,
        "temperatureC": 24.0
      }
    ],
    "ingredients": [
      {
        "name": "Bread flour",
        "unit": "g",
        "category": "flour",
        "quantity": 500.000,
        "isOptional": false
      },
      {
        "name": "Water",
        "unit": "g",
        "category": "water",
        "quantity": 350.000,
        "isOptional": false
      }
    ],
    "loafWeightG": 900.0,
    "yieldLoaves": 1,
    "schemaVersion": 1
  },
  "commentCount": 0,
  "reactions": {},
  "myReaction": null,
  "isMine": false,
  "hasPhoto": false
}
```

</details>

<details><summary>Example — not found (404)</summary>

```http
GET /api/v1/library/00000000-0000-4000-9999-000000000000
```

<a id="addcomment"></a>
### Comment a published recipe

`POST /api/v1/library/{id}/comments` · operation id `AddComment` · auth: bearer token

US-41. The comment is shown with the author's name.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |

<a id="copypublication"></a>
### Copy a published recipe into the own workspace

`POST /api/v1/library/{id}/copy` · operation id `CopyPublication` · auth: bearer token

US-42. Creates an independent recipe. The optional body says, per ingredient of the published recipe, which own ingredient to use instead (same unit) or how much of a new one is on the shelf; without a choice an ingredient is matched to the own catalog by name or created.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |

**Request body:** null or [CopyPublicationRequest](#model-copypublicationrequest)

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 201 | Created; `Location` header points to the new resource | [RecipeDto](#model-recipedto) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 422 | Business rule violated; `detail` says which | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — typical (201)</summary>

```http

{
  "ingredients": [

      "stockQuantity": null

    }
  ]
}
```

## Calculator

<a id="planovenloading"></a>
### Suggest oven rounds

`POST /api/v1/calculations/oven-loading` · operation id `PlanOvenLoading` · auth: bearer token

US-19. Splits the loaves into rounds within the oven capacity of the kitchen settings (422 when not set); recipes with the same oven time share a round.

**Request body:** [OvenLoadingRequest](#model-ovenloadingrequest)

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [OvenLoadingPlanDto](#model-ovenloadingplandto) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 422 | Business rule violated; `detail` says which | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — typical (200)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/calculations/oven-loading
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "items": [
    {
      "recipeId": "e6e62d43-bafb-462e-8b2d-88853bb427c2",
      "loaves": 3
    },
    {
      "recipeId": "6bc19792-0788-44ae-a566-fd54b16ceb23",
      "loaves": 2
    }
  ]
}
```

Response `200` `application/json`:

```json
{
  "ovenCapacityLoaves": 4,
  "totalLoaves": 5,
  "rounds": [
    {
      "round": 1,
      "loaves": 4,
      "bakeMinutes": 45,
      "startOffsetMinutes": 0,
      "items": [
        {
          "recipeId": "e6e62d43-bafb-462e-8b2d-88853bb427c2",
          "title": "Country sourdough",
          "loaves": 3
        },
        {
          "recipeId": "6bc19792-0788-44ae-a566-fd54b16ceb23",
          "title": "Country sourdough, longer bulk",
          "loaves": 1
        }
      ]
    },
    {
      "round": 2,
      "loaves": 1,
      "bakeMinutes": 45,
      "startOffsetMinutes": 45,
      "items": [
        {
          "recipeId": "6bc19792-0788-44ae-a566-fd54b16ceb23",
          "title": "Country sourdough, longer bulk",
          "loaves": 1
        }
      ]
    }
  ],
  "totalOvenMinutes": 90
}
```

</details>

US-14, US-15, US-47, US-48, US-49. Lines from fn_recipe_cost; total = sum of the rounded lines.

| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

## Auth

<a id="register"></a>
### Register a new account

`POST /api/v1/auth/register` · operation id `Register` · auth: none

US-01. Creates the user and an empty workspace (kitchen settings with defaults).

**Request body:** [RegisterRequest](#model-registerrequest)

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 201 | Created; `Location` header points to the new resource | [RegisterResponse](#model-registerresponse) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 409 | Conflict with the current state (duplicate, in use, already decided) | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 429 | Rate limit or budget reached; see `Retry-After` | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — typical (201)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/auth/register
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "email": "docs.baker.588c65fcd@example.com",
```

<a id="login"></a>
### Log in and get an access token

`POST /api/v1/auth/login` · operation id `Login` · auth: none

US-02. Returns an RS256 JWT and sets the refresh token as an HttpOnly cookie. Unknown e-mail and wrong password give the same 401.

**Request body:** [LoginRequest](#model-loginrequest)

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [LoginResponse](#model-loginresponse) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 429 | Rate limit or budget reached; see `Retry-After` | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — typical (200)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/auth/login
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "email": "docs.baker.588c65fcd@example.com",
  "password": "Rye-Bread-2026!"
}
```

Response `200` `application/json`:

```json
{
  "accessToken": "eyJhbGciOiJSUzI1NiIs...",
  "expiresAt": "2026-11-25T00:58:02.6432604+00:00",
  "user": {
    "id": "2274e188-d337-453a-b5fb-d8394a0d2447",
    "email": "docs.baker.588c65fcd@example.com",
    "displayName": "Docs Baker",
    "role": "user",
    "createdAt": "2026-11-24T23:58:02.371242+00:00",
    "lastLoginAt": "2026-11-24T23:58:02.6235879+00:00"
  }
}
```

</details>

<details><summary>Example — wrong password (401)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/auth/login
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "email": "docs.baker.588c65fcd@example.com",
  "password": "Wrong-Password-1"
}
```

Response `401` `application/problem+json`:

```json
{
  "type": "https://oneloaf.app/problems/unauthorized",
  "title": "Invalid e-mail or password.",
  "status": 401,
  "instance": "/api/v1/auth/login",
  "traceId": "00-62f6842e9af60f361ab72d17f00862df-eb18f6ab109c4570-02",
  "correlationId": "10d46a9cc325422390a0768093196fa9"
}
```

</details>

<details><summary>Example — second account (200)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/auth/login
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "email": "leaving.07ea2036@example.com",
  "password": "Rye-Bread-2026!"
}
```

Response `200` `application/json`:

```json
  }

```

<a id="refreshsession"></a>
### Exchange the refresh token cookie for a new access token

`POST /api/v1/auth/refresh` · operation id `RefreshSession` · auth: none

US-02. The cookie is replaced by the next token of the same sign-in (rotation). 401 for a missing, expired or revoked token; a token that was already exchanged revokes the whole sign-in.

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [LoginResponse](#model-loginresponse) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 429 | Rate limit or budget reached; see `Retry-After` | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — without refresh cookie (401)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/auth/refresh
```

Response `401` `application/problem+json`:

```json
{
  "type": "https://oneloaf.app/problems/unauthorized",
  "title": "The session has ended. Sign in again.",
  "status": 401,
  "instance": "/api/v1/auth/refresh",
  "traceId": "00-8e1a2f7bd9b0f1b7f6f56cc81484de98-2e0413bf52b47cde-02",
  "correlationId": "c8dc2a4d703244cdbed6fa1d202a1493"
}
```

</details>

<a id="logout"></a>
### Log out

`POST /api/v1/auth/logout` · operation id `Logout` · auth: none

US-02. Revokes the refresh tokens of this sign-in and removes the cookie; the access token expires by itself and the client discards it.

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 204 | No content | — |
| 429 | Rate limit or budget reached; see `Retry-After` | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — typical (204)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/auth/logout
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

Response `204`:

_(no body)_

</details>

<a id="deleteme"></a>
### Delete the current account and all its data

`DELETE /api/v1/me` · operation id `DeleteMe` · auth: bearer token

US-04. Requires the current password; removes the whole workspace by cascade.

**Request body:** [DeleteAccountRequest](#model-deleteaccountrequest)

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 204 | No content | — |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 403 | Authenticated but not allowed (role) | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — wrong password (403)</summary>

```http
DELETE /api/v1/me
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "password": "Not-My-Password-1"
}
```

Response `403` `application/problem+json`:

```json
{
  "type": "https://oneloaf.app/problems/forbidden",
  "title": "You are not allowed to perform this action.",
  "status": 403,
  "detail": "The password is incorrect.",
  "instance": "/api/v1/me",
  "traceId": "00-5141cfc4afd5aa3cac2f8f3f7119c47e-2ea5bb6c7a6fffc9-02",
  "correlationId": "3e7495f1d5724b41bb184a120094964a"
}
```

</details>

<details><summary>Example — typical (204)</summary>

```http
DELETE /api/v1/me
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "password": "Rye-Bread-2026!"
}
```

Response `204`:

_(no body)_

</details>

## Catalog

<a id="listflours"></a>
### List flours

`GET /api/v1/flours` · operation id `ListFlours` · auth: bearer token

US-11. Paged, ordered by name.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `search` | query | string | no | Part of the name. |
| `page` | query | integer (int32), default `1` | no | Page, from 1. |
| `pageSize` | query | integer (int32), default `20` | no | Page size, 1-100. |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [PagedResultOfFlourDto](#model-pagedresultofflourdto) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — typical (200)</summary>

```http
GET /api/v1/flours?page=1&pageSize=20
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

Response `200` `application/json`:

```json
{
  "items": [
    {
      "id": "1477ce50-150b-409c-95f4-7d2236d4070c",
      "name": "Bread flour T550",
      "flourTypeCode": "wheat",
      "proteinPercent": 12.5,
      "grind": "fine",
      "notes": null,
      "createdAt": "2026-11-24T23:58:03.093237+00:00",
      "updatedAt": "2026-11-24T23:58:03.093237+00:00"
    }
  ],
  "page": 1,
  "pageSize": 20,
  "totalCount": 1
}
```

</details>

US-11. Name is unique within the user.

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 201 | Created; `Location` header points to the new resource | [FlourDto](#model-flourdto) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 409 | Conflict with the current state (duplicate, in use, already decided) | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<a id="updateingredientprice"></a>
### Replace a price

`PUT /api/v1/ingredients/{id}/prices/{priceId}` · operation id `UpdateIngredientPrice` · auth: bearer token

US-12. Corrects a price entered by mistake; another price on the new date is a 409.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |
| `priceId` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |

**Request body:** [PriceRequest](#model-pricerequest)

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [PriceDto](#model-pricedto) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 409 | Conflict with the current state (duplicate, in use, already decided) | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — typical (200)</summary>

```http
PUT /api/v1/ingredients/44e5222a-743e-47d6-bcb1-b263d78ed2b3/prices/7b907278-4871-436b-bd20-3df9736a6785
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "price": 4.5,
  "perQuantity": 1000,
  "currency": "EUR",
  "effectiveFrom": "2026-10-25"
}
```

Response `200` `application/json`:

```json
{
  "id": "7b907278-4871-436b-bd20-3df9736a6785",
  "price": 4.5000,
  "perQuantity": 1000.000,
  "unitPrice": 0.0045,
  "currency": "EUR",
  "effectiveFrom": "2026-10-25",
  "changePercent": null
}
```

</details>

<a id="addingredientrecord"></a>
### Record what was bought: price and quantity

`POST /api/v1/ingredients/{id}/records` · operation id `AddIngredientRecord` · auth: bearer token

Packs of a size bought for an amount: the price of the date is created or replaced and the stock grows; the stock of the ingredient is kept from now on. priceOnly=true records only the price.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |

**Request body:** [IngredientRecordRequest](#model-ingredientrecordrequest)

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [IngredientDto](#model-ingredientdto) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

```http
}
```

<a id="setingredientphoto"></a>
### Upload the photo of an ingredient or its pack

`PUT /api/v1/ingredients/{id}/photo` · operation id `SetIngredientPhoto` · auth: bearer token

multipart/form-data with the image in the field 'file'; JPEG, PNG or WebP (checked by content), up to 5 MB. Replaces the previous photo.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 204 | No content | — |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 422 | Business rule violated; `detail` says which | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — typical (204)</summary>

```http
PUT /api/v1/ingredients/44e5222a-743e-47d6-bcb1-b263d78ed2b3/photo
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
Content-Type: application/json

  "multipart/form-data": {
    "file": "<pack.png, image/png, 29 bytes>"
  }
}
```

Response `204`:

_(no body)_

</details>

<details><summary>Example — not an image (422)</summary>

```http

Authorization: Bearer <access token>
Content-Type: application/json

  "multipart/form-data": {
    "file": "<notes.png, image/png, 10 bytes>"
  }
}
```

Response `422` `application/problem+json`:

```json
{
  "type": "https://oneloaf.app/problems/business-rule",
  "title": "The request violates a business rule.",
  "status": 422,
  "detail": "Only JPEG, PNG and WebP images are accepted.",

  "traceId": "00-7959a572f996bd92c76f70023c12f880-8ba2a1dc61002848-02",
  "correlationId": "87e3587df5cc419fa43ec39d3c43b900"
}
```

</details>

## Recipes

<a id="listrecipes"></a>
### List recipes

`GET /api/v1/recipes` · operation id `ListRecipes` · auth: bearer token

US-05, US-37. Filter by title part, archived flag and minimum flour protein; paged.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `search` | query | string | no | Part of the title. |
| `includeArchived` | query | boolean, default `false` | no | Include archived recipes. |
| `minProteinPercent` | query | number (double) | no | Only recipes with a flour of at least this protein (US-37). |
| `page` | query | integer (int32), default `1` | no | Page, from 1. |
| `pageSize` | query | integer (int32), default `20` | no | Page size, 1-100. |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [PagedResultOfRecipeSummaryDto](#model-pagedresultofrecipesummarydto) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — typical (200)</summary>

```http
GET /api/v1/recipes?search=country&page=1&pageSize=20
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

```json
      "yieldLoaves": 1,

      "hasPhoto": false
    }
  ],
  "page": 1,
  "pageSize": 20,
  "totalCount": 1
}
```

</details>

```http
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

Response `200` `application/json`:

<a id="createrecipe"></a>
### Create a recipe with ingredients and stages

`POST /api/v1/recipes` · operation id `CreateRecipe` · auth: bearer token

US-05, US-09. Saved in one transaction.

**Request body:** [RecipeRequest](#model-reciperequest)

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 201 | Created; `Location` header points to the new resource | [RecipeDto](#model-recipedto) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — typical (201)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/recipes
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "title": "Country sourdough",
  "description": "Open crumb, crisp crust.",
  "yieldLoaves": 1,
  "loafWeightG": 900,
  "notes": null,
  "ingredients": [
    {
      "ingredientId": "ecf99f68-e072-43a8-98d4-1864be976247",
      "quantity": 500
    },
    {
      "ingredientId": "99fc2c1d-3cc7-49d0-b22f-716c67323a35",
      "quantity": 350
    },
    {
      "ingredientId": "06cd16db-2357-4039-acf9-a733c76384e4",
      "quantity": 10
    },
    {
      "ingredientId": "372ea90e-42da-4ed8-b537-6b9cfd8da9c6",
      "quantity": 100
    }
  ],
  "stages": [
    {
      "name": "Mix",
      "stageTypeCode": "mix",
      "minDurationMinutes": 15,
      "maxDurationMinutes": 20,
      "activeMinutes": 15
    },
    {
      "name": "Bulk fermentation",
      "stageTypeCode": "bulk_fermentation",
      "minDurationMinutes": 240,
      "maxDurationMinutes": 300,
      "temperatureC": 24
    },
    {
      "name": "Shape",
      "stageTypeCode": "shape",
      "minDurationMinutes": 10,
      "maxDurationMinutes": 15,
      "activeMinutes": 10
    },
    {
      "name": "Cold proof",
      "stageTypeCode": "proof",
      "minDurationMinutes": 600,
      "maxDurationMinutes": 720,
      "proofMode": "cold",
      "temperatureC": 4
    },
    {
      "name": "Bake",
      "stageTypeCode": "bake",
      "minDurationMinutes": 40,

    }
  ]
}
```

<a id="createrecipedraft"></a>
### Create a recipe with ingredients given by name

`POST /api/v1/recipes/drafts` · operation id `CreateRecipeDraft` · auth: bearer token

US-34. Used when a recipe proposed by the assistant is confirmed: ingredients of the user are matched by name, missing ones are created without a price; one transaction.

**Request body:** [RecipeDraftRequest](#model-recipedraftrequest)

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 201 | Created; `Location` header points to the new resource | [RecipeDraftResponse](#model-recipedraftresponse) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — recipe proposed by the assistant (201)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/recipes/drafts
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "title": "Beginner sourdough",
  "description": "A simple first loaf.",
  "yieldLoaves": 1,
  "loafWeightG": 900,
  "notes": null,
  "ingredients": [
    {
      "name": "Whole wheat flour",

      "note": null
    },
    {

      "unitCode": "g",
      "quantity": 15,
      "isOptional": true,
      "note": null
    }
  ],
  "stages": [
    {

      "maxDurationMinutes": 15
    },
    {
      "name": "Bake",
      "stageTypeCode": "bake",
      "minDurationMinutes": 40,
      "maxDurationMinutes": 45
    }
  ]
}
```

<a id="getrecipe"></a>
### Get the effective recipe

`GET /api/v1/recipes/{id}` · operation id `GetRecipe` · auth: bearer token

US-05, US-07. A variant is shown with the inherited content resolved (isInherited).

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [RecipeDto](#model-recipedto) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<a id="updaterecipe"></a>
### Replace the content of a recipe

`PUT /api/v1/recipes/{id}` · operation id `UpdateRecipe` · auth: bearer token

US-05, US-07. For a variant only the differences from the parent are stored.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |

**Request body:** [RecipeRequest](#model-reciperequest)

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [RecipeDto](#model-recipedto) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 422 | Business rule violated; `detail` says which | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<a id="deleterecipe"></a>
### Delete a recipe

`DELETE /api/v1/recipes/{id}` · operation id `DeleteRecipe` · auth: bearer token

US-05. A recipe with variants or used in batches cannot be deleted (409); archive it instead.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 204 | No content | — |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 409 | Conflict with the current state (duplicate, in use, already decided) | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

```http
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

Response `409` `application/problem+json`:

```json
  "detail": "The recipe has variants or is used in batches; archive it instead.",

}
```

<a id="setrecipephoto"></a>
### Upload the photo of a recipe

`PUT /api/v1/recipes/{id}/photo` · operation id `SetRecipePhoto` · auth: bearer token

multipart/form-data with the image in the field 'file'; JPEG, PNG or WebP (checked by content), up to 5 MB. Replaces the previous photo. A variant has its own photo.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 204 | No content | — |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 422 | Business rule violated; `detail` says which | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — typical (204)</summary>

```http
PUT /api/v1/recipes/e6e62d43-bafb-462e-8b2d-88853bb427c2/photo
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "multipart/form-data": {
    "file": "<loaf.png, image/png, 29 bytes>"
  }
}
```

Response `204`:

_(no body)_

</details>

## Production

<a id="listbatches"></a>
### Production calendar

`GET /api/v1/batches` · operation id `ListBatches` · auth: bearer token

US-26. Batches by planned start, optionally filtered by period and status; paged.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `from` | query | string (date-time) | no | Planned start from (inclusive). |
| `to` | query | string (date-time) | no | Planned start to (exclusive). |
| `status` | query | string | no | Only this status: planned, in_progress, done or cancelled. |
| `page` | query | integer (int32), default `1` | no | Page, from 1. |
| `pageSize` | query | integer (int32), default `20` | no | Page size, 1-100. |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [PagedResultOfBatchSummaryDto](#model-pagedresultofbatchsummarydto) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — by status (200)</summary>

```http
GET /api/v1/batches?status=planned
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

Response `200` `application/json`:

```json
{
  "items": [
    {
      "id": "3db01792-317f-4561-9dbf-92efc8f4bcdb",
      "name": "Weekend bake",
      "status": "planned",
      "plannedStartAt": "2026-11-26T06:00:00+00:00",
      "plannedReadyFrom": "2026-11-26T21:05:00+00:00",
      "plannedReadyTo": "2026-11-27T00:20:00+00:00",
      "totalLoaves": 3
    }
  ],
  "page": 1,
  "pageSize": 20,
  "totalCount": 1
}
```

</details>

```http
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

```http
  "name": "Weekend bake",
  "plannedStartAt": "2026-11-26T08:00:00+02:00",
  "source": "manual",
  "items": []
}
```

Response `400` `application/problem+json`:

```json

  "title": "One or more validation errors occurred.",
  "status": 400,
  "instance": "/api/v1/batches",
  "errors": {
    "items": [
      "'Items' должно быть заполнено."
    ]
  },
  "traceId": "00-cba6f3a288dc5e4f3f98e357a284abdf-e57e2aca955a789f-02",
  "correlationId": "afde3f0fed474718af8a0d1db6a82ad6"
}
```

</details>

<a id="previewbatch"></a>
### Plan a batch without saving it

`POST /api/v1/batches/preview` · operation id `PreviewBatch` · auth: bearer token

US-34. Same input as create; the plan is built by the database and rolled back. Ids are empty. The stock check tells which ingredients are missing and what the stock is enough for.

**Request body:** [BatchRequest](#model-batchrequest)

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [BatchDto](#model-batchdto) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — typical (200)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/batches/preview

{

  "plannedStartAt": "2026-11-26T08:00:00+02:00",

      "remindToFeedStarter": true
    }
  ]
}
```

<a id="getbatch"></a>

`GET /api/v1/batches/{id}` · operation id `GetBatch` · auth: bearer token

US-20, US-23.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|

<a id="replanbatch"></a>
### Re-plan a planned batch

`PUT /api/v1/batches/{id}` · operation id `ReplanBatch` · auth: bearer token

US-20. Only a batch that has not started can be changed (409 otherwise).

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |

**Request body:** [BatchRequest](#model-batchrequest)

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [BatchDto](#model-batchdto) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 409 | Conflict with the current state (duplicate, in use, already decided) | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — move the start (200)</summary>

```http
PUT /api/v1/batches/3db01792-317f-4561-9dbf-92efc8f4bcdb
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "name": "Weekend bake",
  "plannedStartAt": "2026-11-26T09:00:00+02:00",
  "source": "manual",
  "items": [
    {
      "recipeId": "e6e62d43-bafb-462e-8b2d-88853bb427c2",
      "loaves": 3,
      "starterId": "9d2f63d3-eac7-4a52-a3a6-bae64c20507f",
      "remindToFeedStarter": true
    }
  ]
}
```

<a id="deletebatch"></a>
### Delete a batch

`DELETE /api/v1/batches/{id}` · operation id `DeleteBatch` · auth: bearer token

A running batch cannot be deleted (409). The history record of a completed batch stays. ingredientsUsed=false returns the ingredients the batch took to the stock.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |
| `ingredientsUsed` | query | boolean, default `true` | no |  |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 204 | No content | — |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 409 | Conflict with the current state (duplicate, in use, already decided) | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — cancelled batch (204)</summary>

```http
DELETE /api/v1/batches/00b4cd00-2597-42fc-b3b7-c53dc8de859e?ingredientsUsed=false
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

Response `204`:

_(no body)_

</details>

<details><summary>Example — running batch (409)</summary>

```http
DELETE /api/v1/batches/e4be33d1-fb4b-4aba-885c-903dfcef15ea
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

Response `409` `application/problem+json`:

```json
{
  "type": "https://oneloaf.app/problems/conflict",
  "title": "The request conflicts with existing data.",
  "status": 409,
  "detail": "Complete or cancel the running batch before deleting it.",
  "instance": "/api/v1/batches/e4be33d1-fb4b-4aba-885c-903dfcef15ea",
  "traceId": "00-00da19ee9d5bf14b8a8c0e7e95dee5b5-622fdc3564d3ef7b-02",
  "correlationId": "4fbedaf5f4094ca89f4ec85402ef684d"
}
```

</details>

<a id="cancelbatch"></a>
### Cancel a batch

`POST /api/v1/batches/{id}/cancel` · operation id `CancelBatch` · auth: bearer token

Scheduled reminders of the batch are cancelled. ingredientsUsed=false returns the ingredients the batch took to the stock; the default keeps them consumed.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |
| `ingredientsUsed` | query | boolean, default `true` | no |  |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 204 | No content | — |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 409 | Conflict with the current state (duplicate, in use, already decided) | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — untouched batch, ingredients returned to the stock (204)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/batches/00b4cd00-2597-42fc-b3b7-c53dc8de859e/cancel?ingredientsUsed=false
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

Response `204`:

_(no body)_

</details>

<details><summary>Example — typical (204)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/batches/e4be33d1-fb4b-4aba-885c-903dfcef15ea/cancel
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

Response `204`:

_(no body)_

</details>

<details><summary>Example — not found or another user's (404)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/batches/00000000-0000-4000-9999-000000000000/cancel
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

Response `404` `application/problem+json`:

```json
{
  "type": "https://oneloaf.app/problems/not-found",
  "title": "The requested resource was not found.",
  "status": 404,
  "detail": "The batch was not found.",
  "instance": "/api/v1/batches/00000000-0000-4000-9999-000000000000/cancel",
  "traceId": "00-870993682d143aab0f8250dc72f2ddb1-af0c72b1cf65edfa-02",
  "correlationId": "e773d76b53eb4bf5b33d729b10a6d28e"
}
```

</details>

<a id="completebatch"></a>
### Complete a running batch

`POST /api/v1/batches/{id}/complete` · operation id `CompleteBatch` · auth: bearer token

US-31. The database creates the history record with the cost on the bake date.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [CompleteBatchResponse](#model-completebatchresponse) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 409 | Conflict with the current state (duplicate, in use, already decided) | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — typical (200)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/batches/3db01792-317f-4561-9dbf-92efc8f4bcdb/complete
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

Response `200` `application/json`:

```json
{
  "bakeRecordId": "11656437-e094-4620-a1b1-ce7df5c8c422"
}
```

</details>

```http
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

```json
{
  "type": "https://oneloaf.app/problems/conflict",
  "title": "The request conflicts with existing data.",
  "status": 409,
  "detail": "The batch is already finished.",
```

<a id="savebatchasvariant"></a>
### Save how the batch was baked as a recipe variant

`POST /api/v1/batches/{id}/save-as-variant` · operation id `SaveBatchAsVariant` · auth: bearer token

US-25. Finished stages take their measured duration, changed or extended stages their batch durations; everything else is inherited. 422 when the batch followed the recipe.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |

**Request body:** [SaveAsVariantRequest](#model-saveasvariantrequest)

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 201 | Created; `Location` header points to the new resource | [SaveAsVariantResponse](#model-saveasvariantresponse) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 422 | Business rule violated; `detail` says which | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — typical (201)</summary>

```http

Content-Type: application/json

{

}
```

## Starters

<a id="liststarters"></a>

US-27, US-28, US-30. Last feeding, next feeding and expected peak.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `page` | query | integer (int32), default `1` | no | Page, from 1. |
| `pageSize` | query | integer (int32), default `20` | no | Page size, 1-100. |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [PagedResultOfStarterDto](#model-pagedresultofstarterdto) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

US-27.

| Status | Meaning | Body |

## History

<a id="listbakerecords"></a>
### Baking history

`GET /api/v1/bake-records` · operation id `ListBakeRecords` · auth: bearer token

US-33. Filter by recipe, period and minimum rating; newest first; paged.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `recipeId` | query | string (uuid) | no | Only records with this recipe. |
| `from` | query | string (date) | no | Bake date from (inclusive). |
| `to` | query | string (date) | no | Bake date to (inclusive). |
| `minRating` | query | integer (int16) | no | Minimum rating. |
| `page` | query | integer (int32), default `1` | no | Page, from 1. |
| `pageSize` | query | integer (int32), default `20` | no | Page size, 1-100. |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [PagedResultOfBakeRecordSummaryDto](#model-pagedresultofbakerecordsummarydto) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<a id="uploadbakephoto"></a>
### Upload a crumb photo

`POST /api/v1/bake-records/{id}/photos` · operation id `UploadBakePhoto` · auth: bearer token

US-51. multipart/form-data with the image in the field 'file'; JPEG, PNG or WebP (checked by content), up to 5 MB, at most 10 per record.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 201 | Created; `Location` header points to the new resource | [BakeRecordPhotoDto](#model-bakerecordphotodto) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 422 | Business rule violated; `detail` says which | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

```http

}

}
```

</details>

```http
}
```

## Moderation

<a id="listreports"></a>
### Content reports

`GET /api/v1/admin/reports` · operation id `ListReports` · auth: bearer token, role `admin`

LR-11. Administrators only; open reports by default, oldest first. Requires the admin role.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `status` | query | string, default `"open"` | no | Status filter: open (default), resolved or rejected. |
| `page` | query | integer (int32), default `1` | no | Page, from 1. |
| `pageSize` | query | integer (int32), default `20` | no | Page size, 1-100. |

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 200 | OK | [PagedResultOfReportDto](#model-pagedresultofreportdto) |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 403 | Authenticated but not allowed (role) | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — not an administrator (403)</summary>

```http
GET /api/v1/admin/reports
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

Response `403` `application/problem+json`:

```json
{
  "type": "https://oneloaf.app/problems/forbidden",
  "title": "You are not allowed to perform this action.",
  "status": 403,
  "instance": "/api/v1/admin/reports",

  "correlationId": "b85555f20dd94ea19267bff2f8e1d050"
}
```

</details>

<details><summary>Example — typical (200)</summary>

```http
GET /api/v1/admin/reports?status=open&page=1&pageSize=20
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
```

Response `200` `application/json`:

```json
{
  "items": [
    {
      "id": "4820425d-a548-42ee-b471-800551601f1a",
```

<a id="resolvereport"></a>
### Decide a report

`POST /api/v1/admin/reports/{id}/resolve` · operation id `ResolveReport` · auth: bearer token, role `admin`

LR-11. remove hides the reported comment or removes the publication with the reason; reject keeps it. 409 when already decided. Requires the admin role.

| Parameter | In | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the resource (UUID). |

**Request body:** [ResolveReportRequest](#model-resolvereportrequest)

| Status | Meaning | Body |
|---|---|---|
| 204 | No content | — |
| 400 | Validation failed; `errors` lists the invalid fields | [HttpValidationProblemDetails](#model-httpvalidationproblemdetails) |
| 401 | Missing, invalid or expired bearer token | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 403 | Authenticated but not allowed (role) | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 404 | Not found, or it belongs to another user | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |
| 409 | Conflict with the current state (duplicate, in use, already decided) | [ProblemDetails](#model-problemdetails) |

<details><summary>Example — reject (204)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/admin/reports/dacf1530-c024-4487-ae32-8b346b425348/resolve
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "action": "reject",
  "reason": "The comment was already removed by its author."
}
```

Response `204`:

_(no body)_

</details>

<details><summary>Example — already decided (409)</summary>

```http
POST /api/v1/admin/reports/dacf1530-c024-4487-ae32-8b346b425348/resolve
Authorization: Bearer <access token>
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "action": "remove",
  "reason": "Advertising is not allowed."
}
```

Response `409` `application/problem+json`:

```json
{
  "type": "https://oneloaf.app/problems/conflict",
  "title": "The request conflicts with existing data.",
  "status": 409,
  "detail": "The report was already decided.",
  "instance": "/api/v1/admin/reports/dacf1530-c024-4487-ae32-8b346b425348/resolve",
  "traceId": "00-7dcafc443508d29e5dae54b911994e58-59497cf333eae15e-02",
  "correlationId": "a7738b8323aa465cbc33dfc8cc3faa9a"
}
```

</details>

## Data models

<a id="model-bakerecorddto"></a>
### BakeRecordDto

History record created when a batch is completed (US-31, US-32, US-36).

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string (uuid) | yes | Id. |
| `batchId` | string (uuid), nullable | yes | Batch. |
| `bakedOn` | string (date) | yes | Bake date. |
| `totalLoaves` | integer (int32) | yes | Loaves. |
| `totalCost` | number (double), nullable | yes | Cost on the bake date. |
| `currency` | string | yes | Currency. |
| `rating` | integer (int16), nullable | yes | Rating 1-5. |
| `comment` | string, nullable | yes | Feedback of the baker. |
| `assistantAnalysis` | string, nullable | yes | Analysis by the assistant. |
| `analysisAt` | string (date-time), nullable | yes | Time of the analysis. |
| `items` | array of [BakeRecordItemDto](#model-bakerecorditemdto) | yes | Items. |
| `createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes | Creation time. |
| `updatedAt` | string (date-time) | yes | Last change. |

<a id="model-ingredientrecordrequest"></a>

| `currency` | string | yes | Currency code. |
| `date` | string (date) | yes | Date of the purchase; the price applies from this date. |
| `note` | string, nullable | no | Note, for example the shop. |
| `priceOnly` | boolean | no | True records only the price: the stock does not change and is not switched on. |

| `doughWeightG` | number (double) | yes | Dough weight of all loaves, g. |

| `mergedCycles` | integer (int32), nullable | yes | Cycles when the dough is kneaded together; null when no bowl is set. |
| `explanation` | string | yes | Why the kneads can or cannot be merged. |

<a id="model-stagelogentrydto"></a>
### StageLogEntryDto

Planned vs actual times of a stage (v_batch_stage_log, US-36).

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `batchItemId` | string (uuid) | yes | Batch item. |
| `recipeTitle` | string | yes | Recipe title. |
| `batchStageId` | string (uuid) | yes | Stage. |
| `sortOrder` | integer (int32) | yes | Order. |
| `stageName` | string | yes | Stage name. |
| `stageTypeCode` | string | yes | Stage type. |
| `stageStatus` | [StageStatus](#model-stagestatus) | yes |  |
| `plannedStartAt` | string (date-time) | yes | Planned start. |
| `plannedEndMinAt` | string (date-time) | yes | Planned earliest end. |
| `plannedEndMaxAt` | string (date-time) | yes | Planned latest end. |
| `actualStartAt` | string (date-time), nullable | yes | Actual start. |
| `actualEndAt` | string (date-time), nullable | yes | Actual end. |
| `extendedMinutes` | integer (int32) | yes | Extension, minutes. |
| `actualTemperatureC` | number (double), nullable | yes | Measured temperature. |
| `startDelayMinutes` | number (double), nullable | yes | Actual start minus planned start, minutes. |
| `plannedMinMinutes` | integer (int32) | yes | Planned minimum. |
| `plannedMaxMinutes` | integer (int32) | yes | Planned maximum. |
| `actualMinutes` | integer (int32), nullable | yes | Actual duration, minutes. |
| `deviationMinutes` | integer (int32), nullable | yes | Minutes outside the planned window (positive = longer). |

<a id="model-stagestatus"></a>
### StageStatus

pending, running, done, skipped.

Values: `pending`, `running`, `done`, `skipped`

| `name` | string | yes | Name. |
| `isFermentation` | boolean | yes | Fermentation stage (proofing mode allowed). |
| `defaultUsesOven` | boolean | yes | Default of usesOven. |
| `defaultUsesMixer` | boolean | yes | Default of usesMixer. |

### StartWindowDto

Start window for a desired ready time (US-52).

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `recipeId` | string (uuid) | yes | Recipe. |
| `readyAt` | string (date-time) | yes | Desired ready time. |
| `startFrom` | string (date-time) | yes | Earliest start (with the longest durations). |
| `startTo` | string (date-time) | yes | Latest start (with the shortest durations). |

<a id="model-starterdto"></a>
### StarterDto

A starter with its schedule from the last feeding (US-27, US-28, US-30).

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string (uuid) | yes | Id. |
| `name` | string | yes | Name. |
| `flourId` | string (uuid), nullable | yes | Flour. |
| `hydrationPercent` | number (double) | yes | Hydration, %. |
| `feedingMode` | [FeedingMode](#model-feedingmode) | yes |  |
| `feedingIntervalHours` | integer (int32) | yes | Hours between feedings. |
| `riseHours` | number (double), nullable | yes | Hours to peak. |
| `isActive` | boolean | yes | Active. |
| `notes` | string, nullable | yes | Notes. |
| `lastFedAt` | string (date-time), nullable | yes | Last feeding. |
| `nextFeedingAt` | string (date-time), nullable | yes | Next feeding from the last feeding. |
| `expectedPeakAt` | string (date-time), nullable | yes | Expected peak from the last feeding. |
| `createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes | Creation time. |
| `updatedAt` | string (date-time) | yes | Last change. |
| `flourIngredientId` | string (uuid), nullable | no | Ingredient whose stock a feeding takes the flour from. |
| `waterIngredientId` | string (uuid), nullable | no | Ingredient whose stock a feeding takes the water from. |
| `amountG` | number (double), nullable | no | Starter after the last feeding, g: flour + water + starter kept. |
| `ingredientId` | string (uuid), nullable | no | The starter as an ingredient of the catalog: recipes use it, its stock is the ripe starter. |
| `usableShare` | number (double) | no | Share of the fed starter that becomes stock when it is ripe. |
| `usableG` | number (double), nullable | no | Ripe starter in stock now, g; 0 while the starter is ripening. |
| `suggestedKeptG` | number (double), nullable | no | Starter left for the next feeding, g: the reserve plus what was not used. |

<a id="model-starterrequest"></a>
### StarterRequest

Body of POST/PUT /starters (US-27).

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | yes | Name, unique within the user. |
| `hydrationPercent` | number (double) | yes | Hydration, %. |
| `feedingMode` | [FeedingMode](#model-feedingmode) | yes |  |
| `feedingIntervalHours` | integer (int32) | yes | Hours between feedings. |
| `flourId` | string (uuid), nullable | no | Flour of the user's catalog. |
| `riseHours` | number (double), nullable | no | Hours from feeding to peak (US-30). |
| `isActive` | boolean | no | Reminders are created for active starters only. |
| `notes` | string, nullable | no | Notes. |
| `flourIngredientId` | string (uuid), nullable | no | Ingredient whose stock a feeding takes the flour from. |
| `waterIngredientId` | string (uuid), nullable | no | Ingredient whose stock a feeding takes the water from. |
| `usableShare` | number (double), nullable | no | Share of the fed starter that can go into dough once it is ripe, 0.05-1; the rest stays for the next feeding. Defaults to two thirds. |

<a id="model-stockadjustmentrequest"></a>
### StockAdjustmentRequest

Body of POST /ingredients/{id}/stock-adjustments: the quantity that is really on the shelf.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `actualQuantity` | number (double) | yes | Counted stock in the ingredient unit, at least 0. |
| `note` | string | yes | Why the stock is corrected. |

<a id="model-stockmovementdto"></a>
### StockMovementDto

A row of the stock ledger of an ingredient.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string (uuid) | yes | Id. |
| `type` | [StockMovementType](#model-stockmovementtype) | yes |  |
| `quantity` | number (double) | yes | Signed quantity in the ingredient unit: positive adds to the stock. |
| `occurredOn` | string (date) | yes | Date of the movement. |
| `batchId` | string (uuid), nullable | yes | Batch that took or returned the quantity; null after the batch is deleted. |
| `batchName` | string, nullable | yes | Name of that batch. |
| `packCount` | integer (int32), nullable | yes | Purchase: number of packs. |
| `packSize` | number (double), nullable | yes | Purchase: content of one pack in the ingredient unit. |
| `totalCost` | number (double), nullable | yes | Purchase: amount paid. |
| `currency` | string, nullable | yes | Purchase: currency. |
| `note` | string, nullable | yes | Note; always present for a correction. |
| `createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes | When the row was written. |

<a id="model-stockmovementtype"></a>
### StockMovementType

purchase, batch_consumption, batch_return, starter_feeding or adjustment.

Values: `purchase`, `batch_consumption`, `batch_return`, `starter_feeding`, `adjustment`, `starter_ripened`, `starter_refed`

<a id="model-warningdto"></a>
### WarningDto

| `issueCode` | string | yes | Kind of issue, for example missing_price or low_stock. |

| `entityId` | string (uuid) | yes | Id of the entity. |
| `details` | string | yes | Human-readable explanation. |
